import numpy as np, re, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
from scipy.optimize import linear_sum_assignment as lsa
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=200)
def H(M,j): return M[64*j:64*j+64]
def Hc(M,j): return M[:,64*j:64*j+64]
w0=W['ln1_w.0']
A=Hc(W['W_o.0'],2)@(H(W['W_v.0'],2)*w0)
Ua,Sa,Vta=np.linalg.svd(A)
FC=W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0']
wl=W['ln1_w.3']
def ica_axes(X,r,seeds=2):
    Xc=X-X.mean(0)
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Xc,full_matrices=False)
    Z=U[:,:r]*np.sqrt(len(U))
    best=None
    for seed in range(seeds):
        ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten=False,max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed,fun='cube')
        s=ica.fit_transform(Z)
        k=kurtosis(s,axis=0).sum()
        if best is None or k>best[0]: best=(k,ica.components_.copy(),s.copy())
    Wm=best[1];s=best[2]
    sg=np.sign(skew(s,axis=0));s=s*sg;Wm=(Wm.T*sg).T
    return (Vt[:r].T/S[:r])*np.sqrt(len(U))@Wm.T,s
def dc(X): return X-X.mean(0,keepdims=True)-X.mean(1,keepdims=True)+X.mean()
U3=np.linalg.svd(W['W_proj.3'],full_matrices=False)[0]
Mvars={}
for r in [24,26,28]:
    T=Vta[:r].T
    un,s=ica_axes(FC@T,r)
    tok=T@un;tok=tok/np.linalg.norm(tok,axis=0)
    P=U3[:,:29]
    Yp=P.T@(Hc(W['W_o.3'],1)@(H(W['W_v.3'],1)*wl)@tok)
    for lam in [0.0,0.1,0.5,2.0]:
        Ypi=np.linalg.solve(Yp.T@Yp+(lam+1e-6)*np.eye(r),Yp.T)
        M=Ypi@P.T@W['W_proj.3']@(W['W_fc.3']*W['ln2_w.3'])@tok
        Mvars[(r,lam)]=dc(M)
english="""the city is located in the northern part of the country on the banks of the river and has a population of about two hundred thousand people according to the census of the year it was founded in the twelfth century by the king and became an important trading center during the middle ages
the university was established in the nineteenth century and is one of the oldest institutions of higher education in the region it offers undergraduate and graduate programs in a wide range of subjects including science engineering medicine law and the humanities
he was born in london and studied at the university of cambridge where he received his degree in mathematics after graduating he worked as a teacher before joining the army during the first world war he later became a member of parliament and served as minister of finance until his death
the species is found in tropical forests of south america where it feeds mainly on insects and small fruits the adult bird has dark brown feathers with a white patch on the wings and the female lays two or three eggs in a nest built high in the trees
the album was released in march and reached number one on the charts in several countries it received generally positive reviews from music critics who praised the production and the vocals of the lead singer the band toured extensively to support the record
the film tells the story of a young woman who moves to new york to pursue a career as an actress the movie was directed by a famous director and stars several well known actors it was nominated for three academy awards including best picture
the railway line was opened in and connects the capital with the port on the coast the station has two platforms and is served by regional trains the line was electrified in the second half of the twentieth century and was later extended to the airport
the team plays its home games at the national stadium and competes in the first division of the league the club was founded by a group of students and won its first championship five years later since then it has won the cup several times
the war began when the army of the empire crossed the border and attacked the town the government sent troops to defend the province but they were defeated in a battle near the river the following year a peace treaty was signed between the two states
the language belongs to the family of languages spoken in central asia it has about one million speakers most of whom live in the mountains the grammar is characterized by a rich system of cases and the verb agrees with both the subject and the object
"""
python="""import os
import sys
import numpy as np
from typing import list optional
from django db import models
from collections import defaultdict
class config object
    def init self name value none
        self name name
        self value value
        self items
    def get self key default none
        if key in self items
            return self items key
        return default
    def set self key value
        self items key value
        return self
def load file path encoding utf
    with open path r encoding encoding as f
        data f read
    return data
def parse args args
    parser argparse argumentparser
    parser add argument input type str help input file
    parser add argument output type str default none
    return parser parse args args
class model base
    def init self args kwargs
        super init args kwargs
        self layers
        for i in range self num layers
            layer self make layer i
            self layers append layer
    def forward self x
        for layer in self layers
            x layer x
        return x
    def fit self x y epochs
        for epoch in range epochs
            loss self loss x y
            if loss is none
                continue
            self optimizer step
            logger info epoch d loss f epoch loss
        return self
def test get value self
    result self client get url
    self assertequal result status code
    self assertequal result data value
    assert len result data
def get queryset self
    queryset super get queryset
    user self request user
    if not user is authenticated
        raise permissiondenied
    return queryset filter owner user
try
    value int value
except valueerror as e
    raise typeerror invalid value s value
else
    result append value
finally
    f close
def to dict self
    result
    for key value in self dict items
        if isinstance value list
            result key x to dict for x in value
        elif hasattr value to dict
            result key value to dict
        else
            result key value
    return result
class testcase unittest testcase
    def setup self
        self db create all
        self user user username test email test example com
    def teardown self
        self db session remove
def compute matrix a b axis none
    n a shape
    x np zeros n n dtype np float
    for i in range n
        for j in range i
            x i j a i j b j
    if axis is not none
        x x sum axis axis
    return np sqrt np dot x x t
def send request url method get headers none params none timeout
    response requests request method url headers headers params params timeout timeout
    if response status code
        raise httperror response text
    return json loads response content
class userserializer serializers modelserializer
    class meta
        model user
        fields id username email
    def validate self attrs
        if attrs password attrs confirm
            raise validationerror passwords do not match
        return attrs
def main
    args parse args
    logging basicconfig level logging info
    for filename in os listdir args input
        if not filename endswith py
            continue
        path os path join args input filename
        print path
if name main
    main
"""
romaji="""wagahai wa neko de aru namae wa mada nai doko de umareta ka tonto kentou ga tsukanu nani demo usugurai jimejime shita tokoro de nyaa nyaa naite ita koto dake wa kioku shite iru
wagahai wa koko de hajimete ningen to iu mono wo mita shikamo ato de kiku to sore wa shosei to iu ningen chuu de ichiban douaku na shuzoku de atta sou da
merosu wa gekido shita kanarazu kano jachi boogyaku no ou wo nozokanakereba naranu to ketsui shita merosu ni wa seiji ga wakaranu merosu wa mura no bokujin de aru fue wo fuki hitsuji to asonde kurashite kita
watakushi wa sono hito wo tsune ni sensei to yonde ita dakara koko de mo tada sensei to kaku dake de honmyou wa uchiakenai kore wa seken wo habakaru enryo to iu yori mo sono hou ga watakushi ni totte shizen dakara de aru
kunizakai no nagai tonneru wo nukeru to yukiguni de atta yoru no soko ga shiroku natta shingoujo ni kisha ga tomatta
aru hi no koto de gozaimasu oshakasama wa gokuraku no hasu ike no fuchi wo hitori de burabura oaruki ni natte irasshaimashita
haha wa sono toki watakushi no kao wo mite nani ka iitage ni shimashita ga nanimo iwazu ni heya wo dete ikimashita watakushi wa hitori de mado no soto wo nagamete orimashita niwa ni wa shiroi hana ga saite ite tsuki no hikari ga shizuka ni sashite imashita
sono ban kare wa osoku made shigoto wo shite ita ga yagate tsukarete nemutte shimatta asa ni naru to ame ga futte ita kare wa kasa wo motte ie wo deta michi ni wa hito no sugata mo naku tooku de inu no hoeru koe ga kikoete ita
kanojo wa nani mo iwanakatta tada damatte watakushi no te wo nigitte ita sono te wa tsumetakatta watakushi wa nanto itte yoi ka wakarazu shibaraku no aida sono mama tatte ita
ojiisan wa yama e shibakari ni obaasan wa kawa e sentaku ni ikimashita suru to kawa no kami kara ookina momo ga donburako donburako to nagarete kimashita
ano toki no koto wo omoidasu to ima de mo mune ga itaku naru ano hito wa doushite ano you na koto wo itta no darou ka watakushi ni wa dou shite mo wakaranai no de aru
gakkou no kaeri ni tomodachi to issho ni kawa no hotori wo aruite ita to kyuu ni tooku no hou kara kane no oto ga kikoete kita minna wa bikkuri shite tachidomatta
sensei wa watakushi no kao wo jitto mite ita ga yagate shizuka ni kuchi wo hiraite kimi wa mada wakai kara shikata ga nai to itta
"""
VOC="\n "+"abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"
def norm(t):
    out=[]
    for ln in t.lower().split('\n'):
        ind=len(ln)-len(ln.lstrip(' '))
        b_=re.sub(r'[^a-z]+',' ',ln.strip()).strip()
        if b_: out.append(' '*ind+b_)
    return '\n'.join(out)+'\n'
def spec(X,k=9):
    ev=np.linalg.eigvals(X);ev=ev[np.argsort(-np.abs(ev))][:k];return ev/np.abs(ev[0])
refs={}
for nm,tx in [('english',english),('python',python),('romaji',romaji)]:
    t=norm(tx);ids=[VOC.index(c) for c in t];f=np.bincount(ids,minlength=28)
    for keep_n in [20,23,26]:
        for al in [0.3,1.0]:
            keep=np.argsort(-f)[:keep_n]
            C=np.full((28,28),al)
            for a,b in zip(ids[:-1],ids[1:]): C[b,a]+=1
            Ck=C[np.ix_(keep,keep)];pj=Ck/Ck.sum()
            L=dc(np.log(pj/(pj.sum(0,keepdims=True)*pj.sum(1,keepdims=True))))
            refs[(nm,keep_n,al)]=spec(L)
    print(nm,'spec',np.round(refs[(nm,23,0.3)],2))
for key in [(28,0.0),(28,0.5),(26,0.1)]:
    print('M',key,np.round(spec(Mvars[key]),2))
def dist(a,b):
    D=np.abs(a[:,None]-b[None,:]);r,c=lsa(D);return D[r,c].mean()
print('mean distance of M variants to refs')
tab={}
for mk,MM in Mvars.items():
    s=spec(MM)
    row={nm:np.mean([dist(s,refs[k]) for k in refs if k[0]==nm]) for nm in ['english','python','romaji']}
    tab[mk]=row
    print(mk,{k:round(float(v),3) for k,v in row.items()},'best',min(row,key=row.get))

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
import scipy.linalg as sl
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=210)
def H(M,j): return M[64*j:64*j+64]
def Hc(M,j): return M[:,64*j:64*j+64]
w0=W['ln1_w.0']
Q2=H(W['W_q.0'],2)*w0;K2=H(W['W_k.0'],2)*w0
B2=Q2.T@K2/8
ev,Vl,Vr=sl.eig(B2,left=True,right=True)
idx=np.argsort(-np.abs(ev))
planes=[]
seen=[]
for i in idx[:40]:
    if ev[i].imag<-1e-9: continue
    r=Vr[:,i];l=Vl[:,i]
    if abs(ev[i].imag)>1e-9:
        Mx=np.c_[r.real,r.imag,l.real,l.imag]
        Uu,Ss,_=np.linalg.svd(Mx,full_matrices=False);E=Uu[:,:2]
    else:
        Mx=np.c_[r.real,l.real];Uu,Ss,_=np.linalg.svd(Mx,full_matrices=False);E=Uu[:,:1]
    planes.append((np.degrees(np.angle(ev[i])),abs(ev[i]),E))
Mh=sum(Hc(W['W_o.0'],j)@(H(W['W_v.0'],j)*w0) for j in range(6))
print('plane: H2 phase, |ev|, output gain into x (|M E|)')
for ph,m,E in planes[:16]:
    print(f'{ph:7.1f} {m:.3f} gain',np.linalg.svd(Mh@E,compute_uv=False).round(2))
def phase(R):
    if R.shape[0]==1: return 0.0,float(R[0,0])
    a=(R[0,0]+R[1,1])/2;c=(R[1,0]-R[0,1])/2;return float(np.degrees(np.arctan2(c,a))),float(np.hypot(a,c))
# orient each plane using H2 itself
orient=[]
for ph,m,E in planes:
    R=E.T@B2@E
    p,a=phase(R)
    orient.append(np.sign(p) if E.shape[1]==2 else 1)
ds=np.arange(0,16)
for l in [0,1,2,3]:
    wl=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']
    for j in range(6):
        Q=H(W[f'W_q.{l}'],j)*wl;K=H(W[f'W_k.{l}'],j)*wl
        B=Q.T@K/8
        ker=np.zeros(len(ds));info=[]
        for (ph,m,E),o in zip(planes[:14],orient):
            Y=E if l==0 else (Mh@E)+E
            R=Y.T@B@Y
            p,a=phase(R)
            p=o*p
            om=np.radians(abs(ph))
            ker+=a*np.cos(om*ds-np.radians(p))
            info.append((round(abs(ph)),round(p),round(a,2)))
        print(f'L{l}H{j} kernel(d=0..15)',(ker/np.abs(ker).max()).round(2),' top planes',sorted(info,key=lambda t:-t[2])[:3])

import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=200)
def H(M,j): return M[64*j:64*j+64]
def Hc(M,j): return M[:,64*j:64*j+64]

P=np.linalg.svd(W['W_proj.3'],full_matrices=False)[0][:,:29]
f=lambda X:np.trace(X)/(np.linalg.norm(X)*np.sqrt(X.shape[0])+1e-12)
print('head  |Q| |K| |V|  tr(B)  sym(cur,cur) cur>H2 H2>cur H2>L1A L1A>H2 L1c>L1c L1c>L1A L1A>L1c  O-in-P')
for l in [1,2,3]:
    wl=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']
    for j in range(6):
        Qr=H(W[f'W_q.{l}'],j);Kr=H(W[f'W_k.{l}'],j);Vr=H(W[f'W_v.{l}'],j)
        Q=Qr*wl;K=Kr*wl;B=Q.T@K/8
        I=np.eye(384)
        v=[f(T.T@X.T@B@Y@T) for X,Y in [(I,I),(I,A),(A,I),(A,L1A),(L1A,A),(L1c,L1c),(L1c,L1A),(L1A,L1c)]]
        Oj=Hc(W[f'W_o.{l}'],j)
        print(f'L{l}H{j} {np.linalg.norm(Qr):5.1f} {np.linalg.norm(Kr):5.1f} {np.linalg.norm(Vr):5.1f} {np.trace(B):6.2f} ',np.round(v,2),round(float(np.linalg.norm(P.T@Oj)**2/np.linalg.norm(Oj)**2),3))

```
